When you go to your Jobs page, you will see a complete list of all of the job ads you have created. Closed or Expired jobs will have $0 in the Avg. Daily Budget column. If you want to promote one of your closed or expired jobs, click on the Status dropdown menu and select Open to make it visible on Monster. Then you will be able to promote the job.

Read our article, Manage Your Monster Job Postings to learn more.

Managing Applicants

When you sign into your Monster Account, click on Applicants at the top of the page. This will bring you to a list of applicants that have applied to any of your job ads. Feel free to use the filters and sorting tools to better manage your list, mark applicants as favorite, indicate your interest level, and set a status to keep track of their progress through the hiring cycle. You can also contact an applicant from this view. To learn more, review our article on Applicant Management.

Resume Search

As many as you want! You can even save some of your searches for easy access in the future. Keep in mind that you can save up to 10 searches with Classic Resume Search. You can view and manage your saved searches from the top of the page via the My Candidates menu.

Yes! You can save your search and receive an email notification when new candidate matches are found. It’s easy! On the left side of your search results page, click “Save search” under your criteria and follow the onscreen prompts to complete your set up.

Yes! At the top of your results page, you will see a “Sort By” dropdown menu. Select “Resume Updated” in Descending order and click “Go” to refresh your results with the most current resumes at the top of the page. Just remember that candidates who haven’t updated their resumes recently may still be open to your opportunity.

Yes, you can block resumes in two ways. You can select the resume (or resumes) as part of a search results listing and choose Block Resume from the Action drop-down menu. Alternately, you can click the Block Resume link when viewing an individual resume. When you block a resume it will be noted as blocked in search results and will be omitted from Saved Search results.

If I block a resume, will that resume be deleted?

No. The resume will still remain in our resume database. If a blocked resume is found in your future resume searches, it will be noted with a blocked icon to save you from selecting and viewing the resume again. Also, blocked resumes are not included in your Saved Search email results.

Can I un-block a resume?

Yes. If a blocked resume is returned in your search results, you can click the Un-block link to change the resume’s status.

How many resumes can I block?

You can block up to 1,000 resumes. This is a rolling cap. When resume 1,001 is blocked, the first resume will be automatically un-blocked.

Monster Credits

If you do not want to go beyond the monthly allotment of credits, keep an eye on your credit balance. It’s always prominently displayed in the top navigation when signed in to Monster+. We’ll also send you an email notification when your Credit balance is low so you can make any adjustments to your current promotions or resume search usage.

Since 1 Monster Credit = $1, you can expect to use the same number of credits as the promotion prices you select. When it comes to Resume Search, each chargeable candidate action will deduct 2 Monster Credit.

You need to have a Monster+ Pro subscription to receive an allotment of Monster Credits each month. Log into Monster+ and click on the credit icon at the top of the page to learn more. Then, click the Upgrade button to purchase the Pro plan.

When signed in to Monster+, your Monster Credit balance is prominently featured at the top of the page. Click on it to view your spending history. This number refreshes about every 30 minutes, so it should be a great reference point when considering budget and usage.

Negative balances are ok – it’s just how we track billable actions once you’ve depleted the credits included in your monthly subscription. When this occurs, billable actions (i.e., setting promotion budgets or emailing candidates), are tallied in the negative to represent the balance that will be applied to your next billing statement.

You won’t need to! To make it easy and convenient, Monster+ Pro customers can surpass their credit balance without interruption. Monster will just track any overages and apply them to the payment method we have on file.

Credits must be used by the end of the billing cycle or they will be forfeited. To make use of your credits during a recruitment slowdown, we recommend proactively using Resume Search to identify future frontrunners and improve your candidate pipeline.

No need to worry. If you exceed your credit balance for the billing cycle. Monster will just track any overages and apply them to your next bill.

Just know that after your Monster Credits have been depleted, there will not be an interruption in service and any chargeable actions (i.e., current job ad promotions or resume views) will be tracked and applied to your next billing statement. You will know when you are accruing additional charges if the credit balance is showing a negative number (i.e., -30).

Your Monster+ Pro plan includes Monster Credits that can be used in two ways:

Promote Jobs: Apply Monster Credits to your job ad promotion budget.

Search Resumes: Apply Monster Credits to chargeable candidate actions (i.e., emailing a candidate or viewing a resume)

Monster Credits are included as part of your monthly Monster+ Pro Plan. They are applied to your selected daily average budget and can be used to proactively search and contact candidates in our resume database.

Billing/Payment

It depends on your plan:

  • If you have an Annual Plan with Monster+, contact your Sales Representative for assistance.
  • Monster+ Standard Plan Customers: To stop further charges, simply close your job(s).
  • Monthly Monster+ Pro Plan Subscribers: To stop further charges, you must do two things:
    1. Cancel your monthly subscription.
    2. Close your jobs.

Please note that these are independent actions. Closing a job will not cancel your subscription, and canceling your subscription will not close your jobs.

To cancel your Monthly Pro Plan monthly subscription:

  1. Select Company Settings from the Account menu at the top of the page.
  2. Click to expand the “Monster+ Plan” to review your current enrollment.
  3. Click the pencil icon and select the pay-as-you-go Standard Plan.

You will have access to your Pro Plan features until the end of the current billing cycle. Just know that once you downgrade you will no longer have access to Monster’s Resume Search and additional users will no longer have access to your account.

To close a job:

  1. Go to your Jobs page and review the status column.
  2. Click the dropdown menu and change the status of the job from Open to Closed.

Once your jobs are closed or expired, you will not receive any charges from that point forward. When the billing cycle ends, you will receive an invoice for charges accrued while your jobs were open.

Are you a traditional Monster subscriber looking to cancel? Click here.

Absolutely! To ensure your job promotions run smoothly without interruption, Monster will safely and securely perform a one-time authentication on a new payment method once you initiate the first transaction. This process applies only to customers in the Standard Plan, due to its unique, pay-as-you-go billing structure.

Here’s how it works:

Running a job promotion with a newpayment method will prompt a one-time, $10 authentication charge – immediately returned to you in the form of Monster Credits. As your job promotion gains clicks and applies, your Monster Credits will be consumed first, and the remaining balance will be charged to the newly verified payment method. Just know that the Monster Credits will expire after 30 days.

If you need to change the payment method or billing information associated with your Monster+ account, it’s a quick and easy process.

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Billing Information from the list.
  3. Click on Payment Method to expand the options available to you and make any payment updates.

Your Daily Average Budget is, in fact, an average. The actual budget utilized each day is expected to fluctuate over the course of the promotion’s duration (e.g., one month), due to views, clicks, and applies, as well as distribution efforts to maximize your job ad’s visibility.

Just know you will never be charged beyond the total spend you indicate through your promotion selections (plus tax). This is calculated by multiplying the Daily Average Budget by the number of days you want the promotion to run.

($12 Daily Avg. Budget) x (30 Day Promotion) = $360 Total Spend

When you’re ready to put a special offer to good use, sign in to Monster+, or create a new account. Then, follow the steps below:

  1. Open the menu next to your account name (top right).
  2. Select Billing Information.
  3. Expand Promo Code Redemption to enter your code, then click Apply.

The promotional offer, discount, or credit will be applied to your account and available for use immediately. Just know that Monster+ promotional codes do expire, so make note of the offer details and take advantage of the savings as soon as possible.

You will be charged for promotions that were active during your 30-day billing cycle. Check the dates of your active promotions on your invoice. To review your invoices, follow these steps:

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Invoices to view all of the invoices you’ve received to date.

Please note the date of your billing cycle. The promotion charges are outlined and dated to show what was accrued during that cycle.

If you believe you were charged in error or have questions, reach out to customer service or our Credit & Collections department:

Credit & Collections Monster Worldwide, Inc MonsterInvoiceNA@monster.com Tel: 888-506-6543

Your billing cycle end date is the date your payment is processed, which is stated on your invoice. The start date of your billing cycle would be that same day. To view your invoices, click on the user icon at the top of the page and then select Invoices in the dropdown menu.

Standard Plan customers are charged at the end of a 30-day billing cycle.

  • The billing cycle begins on the date the first promotion is launched, with the exception of former Pro Plan customers who retain their original bill date.
  • Any views/applies accrued within that 30-day billing period will be itemized in the invoice at the end of the billing cycle.
  • Charges will be issued before the bill date if the account balance has reached the maximum threshold of £500.
  • To confirm payment authenticity, a one-time, £10 charge will occur when initiating the first promotion. The funds are then returned in the form of Monster Credits.

Monthly Pro Plan customers are charged at the beginning of their 30-day billing cycle.

  • The billing cycle begins on the date the subscription was purchased.
  • Any Monster Credit overages will be charged on the first upcoming bill cycle day.
  • Charges will be issued before the bill date if the account balance exceeds twice the cost of the monthly subscription.
  • Note: If upgrading from the Standard plan to the Pro Plan, the bill cycle day will align with the day it goes into effect.

If you have an annual plan, please refer to your contract for your billing details.

You can find your Billing Information in the user icon dropdown menu. On the Billing Information page you can confirm and edit your billing contact information, stored payment information, as well as your tax information and exemption status.

For your convenience, Monster will email your invoice to you on the day your payment is processed. Invoices are also stored in your Monster account. To review your invoices, follow these steps:

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Invoices to view all of the invoices you’ve received to date.

Please note the date of your billing cycle. The promotion charges are outlined and dated to show what was accrued during that cycle.

The payment information that you enter is safe and will remain protected by Monster’s PCI-compliant payment processor. It will only be utilized for your active and future job ad promotions.

Currently, you can choose to enter a Direct Debit or Credit Card. In the future, PayPal will also be a payment option.

When you promote you first job ad, we will ask you to set up your preferred payment method. You will be charged initially when your campaign balance due has reached $25. From that point, you will be charged either on a 30-day billing cycle or when the account balance exceeds $500.

During the checkout process, after you enter your payment information, you will be asked if your company is tax exempt. If so, you will need to enter you Government-approved Tax Exemption ID, type, and upload a document of proof. If you don’t have your tax exemption information handy at checkout, you can skip the step and add this information later in the Billing Information page within your Account menu. Just click on Tax Exemption to expand the section and edit your information.

The company name is located in 3 places on your account.

Job Posting Details

When posting a job, you can enter a company name which will appear on the job posting. To edit this company, follow the below steps.

  1. Navigate to the Jobs link located in the top navigation bar
  2. Locate the job company name you would like to change on the job posting.
  3. Under the job title is a menu for Actions. Click the Actions menu and go to Edit
  4. Locate the Job Title and Location section. Next to your company name, click the edit icon
  5. Update your company name and click continue.
  6. Your company name is now updated on your job posting.
  7. Company Profile

Company Profile

  1. Click on your username in the top right of the page to access account settings menu
  2. Click Company Profile
  3. Add/edit your company name

The company name on your company settings is only used for your reference. To change this, follow these steps:

  1. Click on your username in the top right of the page to access account settings menu
  2. Click Company Settings
  3. Add/edit your company name

Browser Settings & Privacy

Our Privacy Policy is designed to inform you of our privacy practices using language that is as clear and concise as possible.

Any of the common technical errors that occur on websites can be solved by clearing the Temporary Internet Files (Cache) and your browsing data (Cookies) within your browser. The following scenarios are often solved by clearing the cache and cookies, reopening the browser, and logging back in:

  • Can’t view a page on the site.
  • Page does not load.
  • Page no longer exists.
  • Can’t login.
  • Page cannot be displayed.
  • You receive an error message of any type

Posting Jobs

As you walk through the job ad process, red asterisks will indicate required fields. Just know that Monster urges all employers to complete as many fields as possible when posting their job ads for improved search rankings.

You can select the Daily Average Budget that works best for your hiring need. A higher budget will give your jobs more exposure and is recommended for hard-to-fill or urgent positions. To help determine your budget, you’ll see daily and weekly pricing estimates, as well as an estimate of how many applications you may receive over the promotion duration.

It’s all about relevance! Read the Elements of Performance article to learn how you can get better results.

Yes! When you reach the Finalize page, you will see the option to add a video about this job or your company. As long as your video is uploaded to Vimeo or YouTube, simply provide the link in the space provided and it will be included in your job ad. To learn more about recruitment videos, click here.

Your Pro plan subscription includes a set amount of Monster Credits each month which are automatically put toward any job promotions you set up. If your promotions continue after your Monster Credits are depleted for the month, we’ll apply any additional credits used to the card on file – which is why you are asked to confirm your billing information during a new job promotion setup.

The skills are aggregated from the keywords in your job description and standardized to offer candidates a quick way to determine their fit for your role. Skills in common are also highlighted for signed-in users, allowing them to easily identify jobs that match their skillset.

This feature aims to help candidates pinpoint more right-fit roles while delivering more relevant applies to employers.

For best results, Monster recommends including relevant keywords that describe desired skills within your job description text.

Candidates search on Monster for job titles, skills, or keywords within a particular location. If your job ad is considered relevant to the search, it will be presented in the list of results. A lot of factors are considered when the system ranks job ads. Read our  Elements of Performance article to learn how to optimize your job ad and improve your position in search results.

By law, employers in New York City (Manhattan, Bronx, Brooklyn, Queens, and Staten Island) are required to disclose salary in their job postings. When setting up your job, if you see the message “Good faith salary range is required by local law,” you must provide a salary range to move forward.

Outside of NYC, the laws on salary transparency vary depending on your state/province, company size, and job type. Although Monster recommends including salary information for all postings, it is up to each employer to determine what compliance looks like for them. Employers should reach out to legal counsel to review requirements for their company.

Stay informed with our Pay Transparency & Salary Disclosure resource and see how employers are benefiting from new regulations.

Managing Jobs

You can now manage your jobs and promotions at any time – right from your Monster+ account. If you’d like to stop a promotion, follow these steps:

  1. Go to the Jobs page of your Monster+ account.
  2. Click on the Status dropdown menu.
  3. Select Closed to end the promotion and remove it from Monster. Keep in mind that any charges accrued before the promotion was stopped will be settled at the end of your billing cycle.

If you’d like to promote that same job in the future, it will remain on your Jobs page. Simply change the Status to Open when you’re ready to continue the search.

Please note that these are independent actions: Closing a job will not cancel your subscription, and canceling your subscription will not close your jobs.

Since 1 Monster Credit = $1, you can expect to use the same number of credits as the promotion prices you select. When it comes to Resume Search, each chargeable candidate action will deduct 2 Monster Credit.

You need to have a Monster+ Pro subscription to receive an allotment of Monster Credits each month. Log into Monster+ and click on the credit icon at the top of the page to learn more. Then, click the Upgrade button to purchase the Pro plan.

When signed in to Monster+, your Monster Credit balance is prominently featured at the top of the page. Click on it to view your spending history. This number refreshes about every 30 minutes, so it should be a great reference point when considering budget and usage.

Negative balances are ok – it’s just how we track billable actions once you’ve depleted the credits included in your monthly subscription. When this occurs, billable actions (i.e., setting promotion budgets or emailing candidates), are tallied in the negative to represent the balance that will be applied to your next billing statement.

You won’t need to! To make it easy and convenient, Monster+ Pro customers can surpass their credit balance without interruption. Monster will just track any overages and apply them to the payment method we have on file.

Credits must be used by the end of the billing cycle or they will be forfeited. To make use of your credits during a recruitment slowdown, we recommend proactively using Resume Search to identify future frontrunners and improve your candidate pipeline.

No need to worry. If you exceed your credit balance for the billing cycle. Monster will just track any overages and apply them to your next bill.

Just know that after your Monster Credits have been depleted, there will not be an interruption in service and any chargeable actions (i.e., current job ad promotions or resume views) will be tracked and applied to your next billing statement. You will know when you are accruing additional charges if the credit balance is showing a negative number (i.e., -30).

Your Monster+ Pro plan includes Monster Credits that can be used in two ways:

Promote Jobs: Apply Monster Credits to your job ad promotion budget.

Search Resumes: Apply Monster Credits to chargeable candidate actions (i.e., emailing a candidate or viewing a resume)

Monster Credits are included as part of your monthly Monster+ Pro Plan. They are applied to your selected daily average budget and can be used to proactively search and contact candidates in our resume database.

Billing/Payment

It depends on your plan:

  • If you have an Annual Plan with Monster+, contact your Sales Representative for assistance.
  • Monster+ Standard Plan Customers: To stop further charges, simply close your job(s).
  • Monthly Monster+ Pro Plan Subscribers: To stop further charges, you must do two things:
    1. Cancel your monthly subscription.
    2. Close your jobs.

Please note that these are independent actions. Closing a job will not cancel your subscription, and canceling your subscription will not close your jobs.

To cancel your Monthly Pro Plan monthly subscription:

  1. Select Company Settings from the Account menu at the top of the page.
  2. Click to expand the “Monster+ Plan” to review your current enrollment.
  3. Click the pencil icon and select the pay-as-you-go Standard Plan.

You will have access to your Pro Plan features until the end of the current billing cycle. Just know that once you downgrade you will no longer have access to Monster’s Resume Search and additional users will no longer have access to your account.

To close a job:

  1. Go to your Jobs page and review the status column.
  2. Click the dropdown menu and change the status of the job from Open to Closed.

Once your jobs are closed or expired, you will not receive any charges from that point forward. When the billing cycle ends, you will receive an invoice for charges accrued while your jobs were open.

Are you a traditional Monster subscriber looking to cancel? Click here.

Absolutely! To ensure your job promotions run smoothly without interruption, Monster will safely and securely perform a one-time authentication on a new payment method once you initiate the first transaction. This process applies only to customers in the Standard Plan, due to its unique, pay-as-you-go billing structure.

Here’s how it works:

Running a job promotion with a newpayment method will prompt a one-time, $10 authentication charge – immediately returned to you in the form of Monster Credits. As your job promotion gains clicks and applies, your Monster Credits will be consumed first, and the remaining balance will be charged to the newly verified payment method. Just know that the Monster Credits will expire after 30 days.

If you need to change the payment method or billing information associated with your Monster+ account, it’s a quick and easy process.

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Billing Information from the list.
  3. Click on Payment Method to expand the options available to you and make any payment updates.

Your Daily Average Budget is, in fact, an average. The actual budget utilized each day is expected to fluctuate over the course of the promotion’s duration (e.g., one month), due to views, clicks, and applies, as well as distribution efforts to maximize your job ad’s visibility.

Just know you will never be charged beyond the total spend you indicate through your promotion selections (plus tax). This is calculated by multiplying the Daily Average Budget by the number of days you want the promotion to run.

($12 Daily Avg. Budget) x (30 Day Promotion) = $360 Total Spend

When you’re ready to put a special offer to good use, sign in to Monster+, or create a new account. Then, follow the steps below:

  1. Open the menu next to your account name (top right).
  2. Select Billing Information.
  3. Expand Promo Code Redemption to enter your code, then click Apply.

The promotional offer, discount, or credit will be applied to your account and available for use immediately. Just know that Monster+ promotional codes do expire, so make note of the offer details and take advantage of the savings as soon as possible.

You will be charged for promotions that were active during your 30-day billing cycle. Check the dates of your active promotions on your invoice. To review your invoices, follow these steps:

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Invoices to view all of the invoices you’ve received to date.

Please note the date of your billing cycle. The promotion charges are outlined and dated to show what was accrued during that cycle.

If you believe you were charged in error or have questions, reach out to customer service or our Credit & Collections department:

Credit & Collections Monster Worldwide, Inc MonsterInvoiceNA@monster.com Tel: 888-506-6543

Your billing cycle end date is the date your payment is processed, which is stated on your invoice. The start date of your billing cycle would be that same day. To view your invoices, click on the user icon at the top of the page and then select Invoices in the dropdown menu.

Standard Plan customers are charged at the end of a 30-day billing cycle.

  • The billing cycle begins on the date the first promotion is launched, with the exception of former Pro Plan customers who retain their original bill date.
  • Any views/applies accrued within that 30-day billing period will be itemized in the invoice at the end of the billing cycle.
  • Charges will be issued before the bill date if the account balance has reached the maximum threshold of £500.
  • To confirm payment authenticity, a one-time, £10 charge will occur when initiating the first promotion. The funds are then returned in the form of Monster Credits.

Monthly Pro Plan customers are charged at the beginning of their 30-day billing cycle.

  • The billing cycle begins on the date the subscription was purchased.
  • Any Monster Credit overages will be charged on the first upcoming bill cycle day.
  • Charges will be issued before the bill date if the account balance exceeds twice the cost of the monthly subscription.
  • Note: If upgrading from the Standard plan to the Pro Plan, the bill cycle day will align with the day it goes into effect.

If you have an annual plan, please refer to your contract for your billing details.

You can find your Billing Information in the user icon dropdown menu. On the Billing Information page you can confirm and edit your billing contact information, stored payment information, as well as your tax information and exemption status.

For your convenience, Monster will email your invoice to you on the day your payment is processed. Invoices are also stored in your Monster account. To review your invoices, follow these steps:

  1. Sign in to Monster+ and click on the dropdown menu next to your account name.
  2. Select Invoices to view all of the invoices you’ve received to date.

Please note the date of your billing cycle. The promotion charges are outlined and dated to show what was accrued during that cycle.

The payment information that you enter is safe and will remain protected by Monster’s PCI-compliant payment processor. It will only be utilized for your active and future job ad promotions.

Currently, you can choose to enter a Direct Debit or Credit Card. In the future, PayPal will also be a payment option.

When you promote you first job ad, we will ask you to set up your preferred payment method. You will be charged initially when your campaign balance due has reached $25. From that point, you will be charged either on a 30-day billing cycle or when the account balance exceeds $500.

During the checkout process, after you enter your payment information, you will be asked if your company is tax exempt. If so, you will need to enter you Government-approved Tax Exemption ID, type, and upload a document of proof. If you don’t have your tax exemption information handy at checkout, you can skip the step and add this information later in the Billing Information page within your Account menu. Just click on Tax Exemption to expand the section and edit your information.

The company name is located in 3 places on your account.

Job Posting Details

When posting a job, you can enter a company name which will appear on the job posting. To edit this company, follow the below steps.

  1. Navigate to the Jobs link located in the top navigation bar
  2. Locate the job company name you would like to change on the job posting.
  3. Under the job title is a menu for Actions. Click the Actions menu and go to Edit
  4. Locate the Job Title and Location section. Next to your company name, click the edit icon
  5. Update your company name and click continue.
  6. Your company name is now updated on your job posting.
  7. Company Profile

Company Profile

To edit the company name on your company profile, follow these steps:

  1. Click on your username in the top right of the page to access account settings menu
  2. Click Company Profile
  3. Add/edit your company name

Company Settings

The company name on your company settings is only used for your reference. To change this, follow these steps:

  1. Click on your username in the top right of the page to access account settings menu
  2. Click Company Settings
  3. Add/edit your company name

At this time, you cannot change the email address associated to your account. We are working on building this functionality in the future. Stay tuned to this article for updates in the future.

Currently you cannot access jobs posted by another user on Monster+. We are looking to build functionality in the future which will allow users from the same company to access each others jobs.

Can I change my email address within my account after I set it up?

  • 10.4.2023
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Can I change my email address within my account after I set it up?

If you created your account using an email address (as opposed to logging in through social media), then yes, you can change your email address using the steps below:

  • In your Profile, click on the pencil in the contact information box.
  • Click on the “Need to update your email address?”
  • Enter your new email address and click on the “Send verification code”
  • Once the code is received in the email address just provided, enter it in the “verify code” box and click on “Update email address”
  • A notification will be shown that the email has successfully been updated.

Again, this is only for accounts created with an email address. If you created your account through Facebook, Google, etc, you can not update your email address this way at this time. This will be addressed in a future release. Alternatively, it is possible to delete your account (How do I delete my account? ) and create a new one with another email address.

What if I no longer have access to the email address on my account?

  • Please contact us at customer service for assistance.

Free resume assessment

  • 15.9.2021
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Q:  I didn’t sign up for a free assessment.  Can you cancel it? The free assessment is an automated process that cannot be stopped or reversed.  During the upload process there was an option to receive this assessment. If you are not interested in the assessment, please disregard the email when it arrives.  However, we strongly encourage you to take full advantage of the benefits the free assessment may provide to enrich the content of your resume.   Q:  Can I receive more than one free assessment? A: Thank you for your inquiry. At this time, Monster is providing one free assessment to their account holders. We’re really glad you found the initial assessment helpful! As a reminder, our experts are here and ready to help! You can view our available packages for purchase, pay online easily, and let us support you further in your job search by clicking here: Link

Q: How does Monster’s resume-reading technology work? A: Thank you for your inquiry.  A key component of the free resume assessment is that it uses smart technology to analyze a resume document and recommend improvements.  We can show you how commonly used Applicant Tracking System (ATS) software will ‘read’ a resume. This is very important because hiring managers and recruiters leverage this technology to determine a ‘best fit’ candidate.  It is also how they weed out candidates from a job pool if the candidate does not fit the requirements of the position they are looking to fill.   The information contained in the “Summary” and “Employer Perception” sections of your free assessment show how your application will be perceived by this smart technology. If you are finding that this information is not how you would like to be categorized according to these commonly used systems, you can work with one of our expert resume writers in order to optimize your resume. The good news is that your resume is scannable, meaning; ATS software can read your resume. However, if it is not categorizing you correctly, you could be weeded out for jobs that you are interested in and have applied for.

How do I apply to a job?

  • 17.5.2024
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Applying to a job through Monster is quick and easy by following these steps:

  • Search for your job!  How do I search for jobs on Monster?  Find a match and review the details.
  • When you find the job that’s right for you, you can chose to apply using your Monster Professional profile or you can upload your resume to be sent to the employer.

Is this your first-time visiting Monster?

  • If so- you will be prompted to sign up for a Monster account (How do I create a Monster Account? ) to complete the apply process.

Do you already have a Monster account?

  • If you are returning, you can start applying! For all jobs with onsite applications, you can click the “Submit Application” and if the job is an offsite application, you will be re-directed to the employer’s site.  Follow the site prompts if any required information that may need updating in your profile is needed.”

Can I see the jobs I’ve applied to?

  • Yes, click on the heart icon at the top of the screen or the “Job Tracker” tab. On this page you can view and track the status of your jobs, including Saved jobs and Applied jobs.
  • Any apply that takes place on the Monster site is tracked and stored here. You can update the status of your jobs, and also record personal notes on each job.

Can I update my application or reapply to the same job once it is submitted?

  • Once you have completed your application and clicked on the submit button for a Monster job (onsite) you are unable to make updates. The apply button on the job posting will no longer be active.  If you are applying to a position that takes you off Monster, you may click on the button however each company maintains their own application process which will vary.

Is there a way to create or add cover letters?

  • No, not at this time.

How do I create a Monster Account?

  • 7.3.2025
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Topics included in this Article:

  • How do I create a Monster Account?
  • What is the Monster Profile?
  • How do I create my Monster Profile?
  • How do I make changes to my uploaded Resume?
  • What should I include in my Profile?
  • What preferences can I choose?
  • How do I adjust my Monster email notifications?
  • How can I delete my Monster account?

How do I create a Monster Account?

Follow the steps below to create your Monster account:

  • Click on the Sign-up button at the top of the page at www.monster.com.
  • Sign in via Email, Facebook, Google or Apple. You can log on via any of these methods as long as the email address is the same on all of them.
  • Follow the on-screen prompts to complete your set up.

What is the Monster Profile?

Monster is working hard to make it easier for you to find the right fit in your next job opportunity. The Monster Profile clearly presents your skills and experience to employers, is easy to update, and allows Monster to match you to jobs and content that align with your career goals.

How do I create my Monster Profile?

This depends on if you have a resume. Once you sign in for the first time, you will be asked if you have a resume. We’ve outlined the two options available to you below.

Option 1: Upload Your Resume This is the fastest way to build your Monster Profile. You can easily upload your resume and Monster will auto-generate your profile in seconds.

  1. Feel free to drag and drop your resume file, or upload it from your device, Google Drive, Dropbox, or Microsoft OneDrive. Monster supports the following file types: pdf, .doc, .docx, .odt, .rtf, .html, .txt, .gz, .zip.
  2. After your profile is automatically generated, review it to make sure all the information in your resume was pulled into in the appropriate fields and double check your contact information and skill levels. You can make any changes by clicking the edit icon to the right of each section.
  3. Click on the box if you want to allow recruiters to contact you, and if it’s all set, click Looks Good.
  4. Your resume will be stored within your Monster Professional profile and can be used when applying to jobs.  If you need to make changes, you can upload an updated resume by clicking on the edit button (pencil) within the resume section of the profile. When uploading your new resume, you can choose to “just upload,” or “upload and overwrite” your existing profile.

Option 2: “I don’t have a resume” If you click “I don’t have a resume,” at the bottom of the upload options, we’ll help you build one! You will be directed to enter your profile information on the following pages. For information on each of the fields, go to this question: “What should I include in my Profile?”

  1. Review your profile entries to make sure all of the information is accurate and double check your contact information. You can make any changes by clicking the edit icon to the right of each section.
  2. If you want to allow recruiters to contact you, make sure to opt in by checking the box. Then, if everything is entered just the way you’d like, click Looks Good.
  3. You will see your finished profile. Edit it at any time and click the Generate Resumebutton to have all your profile information placed into a formatted PDF Resume. It will automatically download to your device.

How do I make changes to my uploaded Resume?

If you need to make changes, you can upload an updated resume. 

  1. Sign into your Monster Account and click on MyProfile at the top of the screen. This will open the Profile view.
  2. You will see your profile information on this page. In the My Resume section, click on the pencil icon to upload and replace the file with a new document.
  3. Note: When uploading your new resume, you can choose to “just upload,” or “upload and overwrite” your existing profile.

What should I include in my Profile?

In order for your information to be found on Monster, it’s important that your profile is complete. Make sure to enter your information into each of the fields described below:

  • Contact Information: Make sure employers know how to reach you by providing your name, email address, phone number, etc. You also have the option to allow employers to contact you via SMS or Text Message (North America Only).

  • Location: This field captures your address, country, state/province, city, and ZIP code.

  • Summary: This is an introduction to you as a candidate. Here, you can highlight your most sought-after skills, abilities, accomplishments, and attributes.

  • Links: Provide a link to your online portfolio, website, or a relevant project or presentation – and don’t forget to add a description and click the ADD button to lock it in place.

  • Skills: List all your relevant skills to win over recruiters. To save time, a list of suggestions will pop up, but you can also enter your own. Once added, use the slider to indicate your skill level as Novice, Intermediate, Advanced or Expert. You can always adjust, delete, or change the order of your skills if needed by using the drag and drop feature . If you provided a resume, your skills will be pulled from your resume and automatically added to the skills section for you. They will be set to the intermediate level by default, so you will want to review and make the appropriate changes.

  • Work Experience: This is where you can enter your complete work history. Include the company name, your job title, location, and job type (full-time, part-time, per diem, other), and start/end dates. To show employers what you’re capable of, add a description of your experience within each position. There is an Achievements section to reference any awards or accomplishments for the associated position.

  • Education: Enter your degree type (high school, college, doctorate etc.), degree name, school name, location, start and end dates, concentration, and description.

  • Work Authorization: Choose which countries you are authorized to work in.

  • Languages: Select the languages you speak from the dropdown menu and click Add. Then move the sliding scale to indicate your proficiency. You can choose Beginner, Intermediate, Advanced or Fluent. If you provided a resume while setting up your account, any information regarding languages will be automatically added to your profile and set to the intermediate level by default, so you will want to review and make the appropriate changes.

  • Certifications: Provide the name, summary, and dates of any certifications you’ve received.

  • Awards: Add any Awards you have received along with a name and a description.

  • Extracurriculars: Show employers what you’re passionate about with a list of any charity involvement, community activities, clubs, and notable hobbies. However, be sure to keep this section career-relevant and concise.

  • Additional information: There are a few items in this section to consider:

    • Willingness to Relocate: Indicate if you are willing to move to a new location for the right job opportunity.
    • Willingness to Travel: Consider what percentage of time you are willing to travel for work and enter your selection.

  • Private Information: You may choose to answer the following, or say I don’t wish to answer.

    • Diversity - Ethnicity
    • Diversity - Gender

To control who sees your information, click on Settings on the top of the screen and then click on the Privacy &Communications tab. Under Visibility to Employers select the status you would prefer. The options available are described below: 

  • Visible to employers - Employers will be able to see your profile in Monster’s database and all your contact information will be visible.
  • Hide name and contact information- Employers will see your profile, but they will not have access to your name or contact information. This information will remain confidential until you initiate contact by applying for a job via Monster. (Note: All content on your resume will be visible, so if you want to remain anonymous, do not include your name and contact info on the resume you upload.)
  • Not visible to employers- This setting will not allow your information to display (profile, resume) in Employers’ search results. (Note: while employers cannot see any of your information, you may still receive emails from employers who send out an email campaign to candidates with certain skills, inviting you to apply to a job.)

What preferences can I choose?

You can enter your ideal job title and location(s).  Monster will be able to deliver more relevant job recommendations and better match you to new opportunities. Here’s how it works: 

  • Click on Settings on the top of the screen and click on the Job Preferences tab.
  • Enter your PreferredJob Titles and Preferred Job Locations.

How do I adjust my Monster email notifications?

You can control which type of messages you receive from Monster. Go to Settings and click on Privacy & Communications to make your selections. Here is an overview of each: 

Email Notifications:

  • Allow Monster to send me relevant job opportunities -Formerly known as Saved Searches, we’ll send you relevant job opportunities tailored to your career goals. This includes Job Alert emails and job of the week recommendations.
  • Send me career advice and content - Get valuable tips on navigating every step in your job search and career path.
  • Send me updates on Monster services and features - Stay up to date with all the new Monster updates relevant to you. This includes emails such as updates regarding your job search, your profile and Monster features.
  • Send me notifications when employers contact me via the Message Center

SMS Notifications:

  • Allow employers to text me with job opportunities– Select this option if you would like recruiters to reach out to you with opportunities via text message. (North America Only)
  • Allow Monster to text me about my latest job matches – Select this option if you would also like to be notified by Monster via text message about your latest job matches.

How do I delete my account?

  • 17.5.2024
  • Knowledge

Article Body

There are a few options to consider below. 

Option 1: Pause my search and save my Profile for future searches If you’re not job hunting now, you don’t have to delete your account. Simply pause your search efforts by hiding your information from employers and turning off any notifications. This will allow you to retain access to your account and all history for future use. 

Follow these steps to make your account private and turn off notifications.

  1. From your desktop, sign into your Monster Account and click on the Settings icon at the top of the screen.
  2. Click on the Privacy & Communicationstab.
  3. Under Visibility to Employers select Not visible to employers. Employers will no longer be able to see your profile or resume.
  4. Further down on the page, turn off all of the options under Monster Email Communications and SMS Communications (grey slider = off). This will stop all messages from Monster or employers regarding relevant job opportunities, career advice and Monster’s services and features.

Option 2: Permanently delete your Profile and lose all of your information. If you delete your account, your profile, apply history and notifications will no longer be available to you, and employers will not find you in their candidate searches.

Follow these steps to permanently delete your account, profile, apply history and notifications.

  1. Sign into your Monster Account and click on the Settings icon on the top of the screen.
  2. Click on the Manage Account tab. (If you don’t have a Settings section, please see below on how to submit a Personal Data Request.)
  3. Click Delete Account and then confirm this action.

Follow these steps tosubmit a Personal Data Request:****

To submit a Personal Data Request, please click on the following link to complete submission of your request: Personal Data Request. Upon submission, Monster will process pursuant to applicable law.

For more information about how Monster collects, stores, uses and shares personal information, please review our privacy notice by clicking here, selecting your region, and clicking on “Privacy Center”.

How do I search for jobs on Monster?

  • 7.3.2025
  • Knowledge

Article Body

How do I search for jobs on Monster? Searching for jobs on Monster is quick and easy !  

  • Enter a Keyword or Title- As you type, a list of suggested titles (type ahead) will appear, and you can either choose from the list or continue entering your keyword or job title.
  • Enter a Location – Enter the location that you are currently looking for and click to add it to the location field.
  • Click on the Search button-
    • This will bring you to a list of jobs based on your search criteria.
    • The left-hand side contains the list of jobs you can use the left-hand scroll to find a job you are interested in.
    • Click anywhere within the job and the right-hand side of the list will provide the job details provided by the Employer. Use the right-hand scroll to move down to see all the important details of the job.

Can I search by company name on Monster? Yes! When you start to type a keyword in the search bar, you can choose if you are searching by job title or company name. Please keep in mind that you have to search for the exact company name in order for a  search to be successful.  

I want to search Monster for jobs nationwide, why are all of my results local? If you are searching without a location and are only seeing jobs near you, this is most likely a browser setting which can be easily disabled. Depending on your browser, you may see a pin icon on the left or right side of the URL search bar. Click to disable the setting and re-load the Monster website to view nationwide search results.

How do I search for Remote/Work from Home jobs?

  • There are a few different ways to search for Remote jobs:
  • In the search box for location, you can put “remote” when entering your search criteria.
  • After conducting a search, you can filter your results by clicking on the “remote” filter at the top of the page and choosing the “Remote jobs only” option.
  • For convenience, we have also added popular search shortcuts, such as the “Work From Home Jobs” which provides a sampling of jobs within your country.  These can be seen on the home page when not logged in. When using the “Work from Home” shortcut, you may choose to further refine the search criteria to narrow your job search.

I forgot my password. What should I do?

  • 10.4.2023
  • Knowledge

Article Body

I forgot my password. What should I do? You can reset your password with Monster by following these steps:

  • Click on the “forgot password” link in the Log In page and you will be prompted to enter your email address and the Monster Password Reset instructions will be sent to you.
  • If you do not see your email shortly you can try the following:
    • Check Spam or Junk Folder: It may have been accidently flagged as spam, so you may want to check your email spam folder. If so, you may want to add no-reply@ses.monster.comto your address book or safe list
    • Try again: Enter your email address into the forgot password page again. Perhaps a typo or a different email address was used when originally setting up your account.
    • Did you create your account through Apple, Facebook, or Google? If so, then an email and password were not required to create your Monster account. Therefore, instead of using the “forgot password” feature on Monster, you will need to click on Continue with Google/Facebook/Apple and follow instructions to reset your password.

Professional resume writing

  • 30.10.2024
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Q: What does “professional resume writer” mean? A: All our professional resume writers are certified and have received specialized training through our professional development programs. In addition, they bring many years of experience as human resources and recruiting professionals in a variety of industries and locations around the country.

Q: Do you guarantee your work? A: Yes. We provide a 60-day Interview Guarantee. If you don’t get called for an interview in 60 days, your resume rewrite is on us. 

Q: Is every resume truly customized? A: Yes. Our resume writers will conduct a comprehensive review of your experience and career goals to craft a resume that reflects your unique value proposition and highlights your skills and accomplishments.

Q: What is the benefit of having a professionally-written resume? A: Even the most qualified applicants can get passed over for positions for which they are a perfect fit because of a poorly written or keyword-poor resume. After all, recruiters and hiring managers will never see a resume that doesn’t pass the online screening filters.   Q: Why have I not received my resume yet? Your first draft should be delivered to you in about a week.   For any questions about your professionally written resume, feel free to reach out to the resume writing team at support@topresume.com.

What are Monster Job Alerts?

  • 18.4.2024
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Monster Job Alerts allow you to create targeted job alert emails that will be sent to you daily.   

To receive these emails:

  • Go to Settings on the top of the page and click on the Privacy & Communications tab..

  • Scroll down to Monster Email Communications and turn on “Allow Monster to send me relevant job opportunities”  (On = Green).

  • Then click on Job Alerts at the top of the page.

  • Click the “+ Job Alert” button to create a new Job Alert.

  • Enter your desired job title and location. You can also check off that you want remote jobs. (see diagram below for tips on setting up your Job Alerts.)

In addition to Job Alerts, you can also control what jobs you see in “Recommended For You” on your Dashboard and in your Profile. Go to Settings at the top of the page. In the JobPreferences tab, you can make selections to get the best job matches in the location(s) you are looking for. Ensure that all of the information in this section is complete by adding your

  • Preferred Job Titles
  • Preferred Job Locations

We will combine this with information from your Apply History and deliver you with relevant job recommendations to better match you to new opportunities. 

How do I stop receiving Job Alert emails?

  • Go to Settings at the top of the page, and click on the Privacy & Communications tab..
  • Scroll down to Monster Email Communications and turn off the switch next to “Allow Monster to send me relevant job opportunities.” (off = grey)
  • You can also go to your Job Alerts, click the pencil to edit, then delete the Job Alert.

  • Allow Monster to send me relevant job opportunities -Formerly known as Saved Searches, we’ll send you relevant job opportunities tailored to your career goals. This includes Job Alert emails and job of the week recommendations.
  • Send me career advice and content - Get valuable tips on navigating every step in your job search and career path.

Why am I being asked to validate my email address?

  • 22.3.2021
  • Knowledge

Article Body

Why am I being asked to validate my email address? Monster continually works to review and enhance the security of our site to ensure our customers are protected.  We have implemented an email validation process that requests your email address that you are using for your Monster account. Monster will send you a one-time validation code that you will enter in the Verify Code field . Once completed, you can continue to access your account.  

If you do not receive this email , you can check your spam folder as often emails are routed incorrectly into this folder. If you do not find it here, you can click on the “Didn’t receive a code?”  and click on the button to have the verification email resent to you. The Verification code expires after 12 hours, so you can use this  process if you have exceeded the time limit.

Why am I receiving emails from recruiters?

  • 7.3.2025
  • Knowledge

Article Body

  • Check your Profile Visibility - If your Profile is “Visible to employers” employers can see your profile and reach out to you.

Q: I have my Profile set as “Hide name and contact information,” why am I receiving emails? A: Employers can view your Profile, but it will be anonymized. They can’t see your name or contact information, but they can reach out to you through Monster. (Note: If you upload your resume and this contains your name and contact info, employers will be able to see this info.)

Q: My Profile is “Not visible to employers”, why am I receiving emails? A: Employers can not view your Profile. However, they can run searches on keywords and send an email campaign to candidates who have those keywords in their Profile. The employer will have no idea who you are, unless you reply to the email you received.

Q: I deleted my account, yet I am still receiving emails from employers. Why? A: Employers most likely saved your resume on file prior to the deletion of the account. Therefore they are still able to contact you. While this is a frustrating experience, the emails should slow down over time.
